A valid repeated preflight appends/fsyncs an `attempt_history` member with `source_condition` and `base_condition` both `activation-policy-invalid`, atomically sets its nonce active before `preparing`, and retains that current condition/action only until push-intent admission consumes it to `none/none`; unavailable observation remains fail-closed, and no prior intent, result, or ledger member is erased |
| `recovery_required` with `activation-policy-invalid/start-new-attempt` and no prior attempt; next `forge push` after an approved exact repair and repeated valid preflight | `preparing` | Append/fsync an `attempt_history` member with `source_condition` and `base_condition` both `activation-policy-invalid`, atomically set its nonce active, retain the current condition/action until push-intent admission, begin a fresh attempt in the same serialized singleton transaction, bind the repaired current HEAD, and never reuse prior rebase intent |
| `recovery_required` with `remote-moved/start-new-attempt` and partial historical `landed_head`; next `forge push` with the locked current checkout still exact clean, including when it equals the preserved top-level intended head | `preparing` | Preserve every prior attempt/result/vector and partial landed head, append/fsync an `attempt_history` member with `source_condition` and `base_condition` both `remote-moved`, atomically set its nonce active, retain that current condition/action until push-intent admission, and run the fixed-tip fetch/rebase; that nonce becomes the rebase operation nonce. The current condition may later be wrapped for recovery, but the ledger-bound identity takes the fresh-attempt exception until the unchanged-tip row appends exactly one new intent member and consumes the condition/active nonce; prior containment cannot loop it back here even when no-op integration deliberately repeats the same OID |
| `recovery_required` with any other ordinary condition/action that, after its required observation, admits a new attempt, including `none/none`; next `forge push` | `preparing` | Apply every higher-priority restoration, retry, landed, or refusal observation row first. Only when the remaining tuple admits a new attempt, append/fsync a ledger member whose `source_condition` records the prior condition and whose `base_condition` is `none`, atomically set its fresh nonce and normalize the current pair to `none/none` with `preparing` |
| malformed/unreadable/symlinked/schema-invalid singleton | unchanged bytes | Exit 2 exact `frozen-chain`; never replace or infer state |

Every side-effect intent is durable first. Complete common-lock plus commit-lock acquisition and singleton re-read precede any new `preparing` write, so concurrent pushes never overwrite one another and commit finalize cannot move main-checkout HEAD/index during the transaction; a new operation never overwrites a nonterminal transaction. After phase 4 lands, commit finalize's own locked transition tuple admits ordinary `committing` only when this singleton is absent or strict-valid `closed`. Its sole nonterminal admission is DM-015's exact approved canonical repair when the strict-valid singleton is exactly `recovery_required/activation-policy-invalid/start-new-attempt`, has null `lock_release`, and has no live or unresolved fence/process; that finalize preserves the singleton byte-for-byte so the next `forge push` binds the repaired HEAD. Every other strict-valid nonterminal record returns exact `state-precondition` with next `forge push`, and singleton corruption returns exact `frozen-chain`. Release of push's acquired commit lock and common lock is one ordered complete rebase-lock release transaction governed by DM-017 `lock_release`; a pending inner release retains the outer portable owner. `chain_id` is null in every push envelope.

### History-mutation activation and hook output

| Committed manifest result | Raw `git commit` | Raw `git push` | Forge CLI producers |
|---|---|---|---|
| upstream/non-Forge | Existing inert/halt-only behavior | Existing inert/halt-only behavior | Unchanged |
| missing mode or `legacy-v1` | Existing marker-or-chain dual-accept, byte-identical | Existing halt-only phase-1 behavior | Legal subject to their own checks |
| `forge-verbs-v1` | Deny with exact raw-commit literal | Deny with exact raw-push literal | Exactly commit finalize, merge finalize, and push remain sanctioned |
| duplicate/malformed/unknown mode | Deny with exact invalid-mode literal | Deny with exact invalid-mode literal | Only DM-015's exact repair-only candidate-bound commit finalize may produce history; merge finalize and history-producing push are denied, while a strict-valid nonterminal singleton admits `forge push` only in closed recovery-only mode for existing identity-bound truth/restoration/release |

The exact new permission-decision reasons are:

```text
forge: operator verb denied — present the candidate and ask the operator to run this via ! (merge approve)
forge: raw git commit denied — use Forge CLI commit finalize
forge: raw git push denied — use Forge CLI merge finalize or Forge CLI push
forge: history mutation mode invalid — repair committed .forge-manifest through Forge CLI
```

The rendered close protocol is exactly:

```bash
bd dolt push
python3 "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/forge/cli.py" push
git status
```

Each line's result is checked before the next; a preceding failure stops the block.

### `drift-check.sh` — mechanical drift contract

On every outcome, stdout is the canonical Drift summary schema v1 object in FR-161 and is byte-identical to the overwrite-on-same-date transient JSON file. When telemetry is available, its `event_prune` member records FR-157's event-stream prune outcome, including a non-fatal housekeeping failure. Exit 0 = all checks pass; 1 = complete summary with findings (still valid input to `/forge:drift`); 2 = precondition/nonrecoverable-configuration/non-housekeeping-execution/primary-summary failure represented in the JSON (semantic review MUST NOT start). A prune failure alone preserves the otherwise-produced exit 0 or 1.

---

## 9. Error Contract
Coordination diagnostics are selected by the first classified cause below. Raw exceptions and unvalidated caller-supplied IDs, paths, or record values MUST NOT appear in output. The only precedence exceptions are these: a diagnostic MAY name a syntactically valid offending pathspec only as an escaped single-line JSON string literal, and a value that fails pathspec field validation remains un-echoed; diagnostics MAY carry engine-allocated `execution-NN` identifiers, run IDs in the FR-250 grammar, chain IDs matching `journal.CHAIN_ID_PATTERN`, and full commit object IDs read from Git or chain state, but never agent strings or raw caller-supplied paths. Journal errors have no authority over chain gates, approvals, merge, or run lifecycle decisions.

| Condition | Surface | Result | Notes |
|-----------|--------|--------|-------|
| Unfilled required region at Gate 1 | `worktree-merge`/`commit` | exit 1, `forge: <region> not configured — run /forge:init` | Fail-closed by design |
| Present `stack-validations` region with no fenced shell cell | policy parser / init / commit / merge / fresh-reviewer applicability | refuse, ````forge: stack-validations region present but contains no fenced shell cell — write one fenced ```bash or ```sh cell per stack category (see /forge:init)```` | Prose and inline-backtick commands are never executable policy; fresh-reviewer INVALID carries this exact inner reason |
| Halt sentinel present | guard hook / skills | deny / stop | Only the operator clears it; audit line appended |
| Marker missing/malformed/stale/mismatched on `git commit` | guard hook | deny, `forge: commit not authorized — run /forge:commit (<reason>)` | `<reason>` is exactly `marker missing`, `marker malformed`, `marker stale`, or `marker hash mismatch`; 30-min TTL |
| `/forge:commit` Step-5 producer command is not FR-055's single standalone safely quoted segment | guard hook | deny, `forge: commit not authorized — run /forge:commit (marker hash mismatch)` | This retained byte-identical denial is the public command-shape bucket; `unsafe-command-shape` may exist only internally and MUST NOT reach stderr or the hook reason |
| Unwaived Python AST assertion finding or detector failure | commit Step 2 | exit 1/2, no commit | Exact FR-144 strings; fresh Step 2 run required |
| Non-Python assertion finding, absent seeded heuristic, or valid per-file waiver | commit Step 2 / review evidence | printed advisory, exit 0 | Never blocks; waiver path/reason remains visible in review evidence |
| Outside a Forge repository | `aggregate-telemetry.sh` | silent exit 0; no CSV written | Preserves the FR-093 non-Forge Stop-hook contract |
| Valid aggregation with at least one telemetry source, including `events.jsonl` with no per-unit Markdown telemetry | `aggregate-telemetry.sh` | requested exact-schema CSV written with exactly one `__decision_totals__` row; exit 0 | `*.md` availability never gates event parsing or CSV creation; malformed/out-of-window-only event streams still produce a zero-counter totals row; Markdown-only input retains its per-unit rows plus the totals row |
| Missing telemetry inputs in a Forge repository, with no `events.jsonl` | `aggregate-telemetry.sh` | explanatory notice; exit 0 | No CSV is required when neither the decision-event stream nor Markdown telemetry exists |
| Missing/malformed CLI argument, unpaired/malformed/reversed window, or read/write failure | `aggregate-telemetry.sh` | diagnostic; exit 2; no success notice | An existing `events.jsonl` MUST NOT take any earlier no-Markdown exit-0 path |
| Scoped mutation timeout/nonzero/survivor | merge after Gate 1 | Gate 3 evidence, merge continues | Applicable FR-140 timeout; timeout kills full process group; never satisfies a gate |
| Malformed `mutation-testing` region | merge after Gate 1 / drift | `forge: executable policy row malformed`, mutation run skipped | Gate 3/drift evidence; never blocks or satisfies a gate |
| Invariant gate failure, timeout, or malformed policy | commit Step 2 / merge Gate 2 | exit 1, the applicable exact `forge: invariant failed (<enforcement-point>): <invariant>`, `forge: invariant timed out (<enforcement-point>): <invariant>`, or `forge: executable policy row malformed` | Committed policy only; diagnostic output capped; hook findings remain advisory |
| Fast-path policy drift | guard hook | deny, `forge: commit not authorized — run /forge:commit (fast-path policy drift)` | Non-ancestor or changed committed policy; independently recomputed |
| Fast-path eligibility drift | guard hook | deny, `forge: commit not authorized — run /forge:commit (fast-path eligibility drift)` | Exact immutable candidate path/tree evidence no longer derives fast |
| Review BLOCK | commit/merge Step 4 / Gate 3 | revision loop | Max 8 iterations, then residual risk + escalate, never commit |
| Review agent unavailable | commit/merge | no commit/merge | Fail-closed (chain rule) |
| Commit/rebase lock timeout (300 s) | lock scripts / `common-lock hold` | exit 1 with holder hint | Retryable after inspection |
| Decision-event writer registration, open, write, short-write, or prune-lock wait failure, including expiry of the bounded 5 s wait | FR-157 emitting skill/script/hook | emitter skips or fails only the append; records a stable non-secret failure code (wait expiry: `event-append-lock-timeout`); preserves the already-delivered primary result and primary exit status unchanged | Advisory instrumentation only; emitters never acquire the prune lock, and an append failure never alters a permission decision, halt result, commit outcome, or exit status |
| `common-lock hold` unavailable, refused, exhausted, or exited without a readiness record (Revision 13; supersedes the retired `flock`-absent fallback row) | worktree-merge | loud failure, no merge, holder hint | Never skip locking; no skill-created lock and no host-capability backend selection |
| Wrapper release outcome is not `ok` / `forge: common rebase lock released` after the push (Revision 13) | worktree-merge | failed merge step, `lock-release-failed`, owner retained | Push already landed; operator inspects the retained owner before further reintegration |
| Empty or malformed eval suite | `run-evals.sh` | exit 2 | Blocks control-class and review-final-floor commits (never vacuously passes) |
| V2 hook corpus or phase-3/4 manifest violates DM-016's exact Revision-10 keys, enums, ordering, 18-additive/130-effective case counts and IDs, v1 reference/hash, artifact inventory, subject paths, eight-slot inventory, or result-binding representation | v2 corpus/manifest validator | non-PASS, no phase-3 shipment or phase-4 activation | Additive v2 cases never copy the 112 v1 cases; malformed layout cannot be treated as an empty or advisory corpus |
| Generation-1 v2 manifest uses `pending-phase-4` outside `push`, `activated-denial`, or `push-last-line`, claims an unresolved PASS, or generation 2 fails to predecessor-bind and replace every pending slot | v2 manifest validator / phase-4 activation | fail closed; no activation and no qualifying FR-242 evidence | `pending-phase-4` is explicit non-evidence. Recording a fictional PASS is forbidden |
| Monitor: declared events path missing | `monitor` | `monitor_error`, exit 1 | Prevented by FR-036 ordering |
| Stale/unknown agent stream | `monitor` | terminal notification | Ambiguity protocol FR-041/FR-042 before any `execution_result` |
| `FORGE_SESSION_PID` is missing or not a positive base-10 integer | all DM-010 lock/marker entrants | refusal, `forge: FORGE_SESSION_PID must be exported as a positive base-10 integer`; no mutation | Existing byte-identical malformed/missing diagnostic; "entrants" is the skill-level validation preceding the lock scripts — `acquire-commit-lock.sh`/`release-commit-lock.sh` internal literals remain byte-identical per FR-055 |
| `FORGE_SESSION_PID` is syntactically valid but dead or same-host liveness is unverifiable | all DM-010 lock/marker entrants | refusal, `forge: FORGE_SESSION_PID does not name a live same-host session owner`; no mutation | Checked before lock or marker mutation; no shell-PID fallback |
| Forge initialization incomplete in committed manifest | workflow / agent launch | refusal, `forge: forge initialization incomplete — run /forge:init` | First-install bootstrap committed policy but activation has not completed |
| CRITICAL drift block present | workflow skill | refusal, `forge: new run refused — CRITICAL drift block present at .forge/tmp/drift-block; operator clearance required` | Applies before run opening; operator-only manual clearance; merge gates do not inspect it; never halt semantics |
| Malformed or missing `drift-config` | drift checker / staleness hook | warning, `forge: malformed drift-config — using defaults (cadence: 14d, retention: forever, event-retention: 400d)` | Continues with all three defaults; never fail-closed |
| Event-stream prune housekeeping failure | `drift-check.sh` | records a stable non-secret code in `telemetry.event_prune.failure`; preserves the exit 0/1 otherwise produced | MUST NOT take exit 2 solely for pruning; telemetry remains available and semantic review proceeds |
| Drift mechanical precondition/configuration/execution failure | `drift-check.sh` / `/forge:drift` | exit 2; checker stdout is only the always-emitted Drift summary schema v1 JSON, then the skill emits `forge: drift mechanical check failed` on stderr/user-response | No semantic pass; FR-164 recovered configuration remains advisory; code 1 remains consumable |
| dcg present but project allowlist inspection/update fails | `/forge:init` | continue, `forge: dcg allowlist update failed` | dcg is an optional integration; absence and failure are non-fatal |
| Dirty implementer worktree at merge start | `worktree-merge` | stop before Gate 1 | Commit via chain or user-approved discard (FR-060) |
| Control-class merge diff without user approval | `worktree-merge` Gate 4 | wait; no rebase/push | Approval bound to candidate HEAD SHA (FR-060) |
| Live commit chain already exists for the worktree at `commit start` | `cli.py` | refusal, exit 1, names the live chain and abort/finalize remediation | One live commit chain per worktree (FR-211) |
| Pre-existing staged content at `commit start` | `cli.py` | refusal, exit 1, offending paths named | Dirty-index refusal; foreign staged bytes never ride into a candidate (FR-211) |
| Out-of-band HEAD movement while a chain is live | `cli.py` any state-advancing verb | `head_moved` journaled with old→new SHAs; verbs refuse until `commit rebase` or `abort` | Diagnosed as out-of-band commit, not chain corruption (FR-213) |
| Model-issued `commit approve` / `commit skip` | PreToolUse hook | deny; message directs the operator `!` path with the authorization ID | Operator verbs never traverse the model's Bash path (FR-217) |
| Finalize outside `authorized`, expired/consumed token, incomplete evidence, or tree-vs-index drift | `cli.py` finalize | refusal, exit 1, exact failed precondition and remediation | Two-phase protocol preconditions (FR-219) |
| Corrupt chain file, irresolvable event/state divergence, or foreign HEAD in `committing` | `cli.py` | exit 2, frozen chain; only `status`/`abort` accepted | Fail-closed internal failure (FR-219/FR-220) |
| Family enumeration encounters an unreadable unrelated chain | implicit commit/merge family selection | emit exactly one `forge: warning — skipped unreadable chain <chain-id> while enumerating <family> chains` stderr line and continue | Chain-ID/family validation keeps the line escaped and single-line; explicit `--chain-id` selection continues to surface that chain's failure |
| Non-mutating `gate run` while a configured mutating gate is pending | `cli.py` | refusal, exit 1, names the pending mutating gate | Machine-enforced gate ordering (FR-214) |
| Unknown or unconfigured phase-1 gate ID | `cli.py gate run` | exit 1, `state-precondition` | Canonical registry and shipped invariant-alias behavior are FR-214/§8 |
| Control or review-final-floor candidate has a missing, malformed, or unverifiable authenticated reviewer-facing trigger | `cli.py gate run fresh-reviewer-evals` / `verify` | INVALID, exit 2, exact `forge: fresh reviewer eval evidence invalid: <reason>` with envelope reason `evidence-incomplete`; chain parks but is not frozen | Trigger failure is required applicability, not zero match or an unknown/unconfigured gate (DM-003/FR-220) |
| Malformed configured changelog command or missing outputs | phase-1 policy parser | fail closed before execution | DM-003 exact one-cell/no-gate/output grammar; no generic mutating registry |
| Invalid review-verdict UTF-8, line grammar, citation, or PASS/finding combination | `review collect` / `review attach` | exit 1, `review-verdict-invalid`; state/evidence unchanged | FR-216; BLOCK without a finding remains valid shipped behavior |
| One above-MINOR merge disposition already awaits co-sign and another above-MINOR disposition is submitted | merge `review disposition` | exit 1, exact `state-precondition` and `forge: review disposition refused — above-MINOR disposition already awaits operator co-sign`; no event or state change | The flag-less disposition-purpose `merge approve` always selects one object; MINOR does not occupy the slot |
| The single reviewer cannot inspect every byte of an oversized master package through the deterministic bounded views | commit/merge review | refusal, exact `forge: review refused — reviewer cannot inspect the complete authoritative package`; no verdict is accepted and a CLI chain remains `evidence-incomplete` | Never substitute shard packages, summaries, verdict aggregation, or quorum |
| (historical, before Revision 17) Gate-1 fingerprint boundary mismatch between the two newest adjacent unvoided current-candidate PASSes | phase-1 `gate-1` | no longer applies: Gate 1 is recorded once per candidate and no Gate-1 records are compared or voided | Chains written before Revision 17 may carry `pair_voided` markers and the `gate_1_pair_voided` event; readers accept them as history and never emit them |
| Merge worktree path does not exist | `merge start` | exit 1, `worktree-missing`; no chain ownership/history mutation | Exact v2 failed precondition |
| Merge worktree is unregistered, symlink-ambiguous, main, detached, default-branch, foreign-common-dir, or non-local-branch | `merge start` | exit 1, `worktree-invalid`; no ownership/history mutation | Candidate policy cannot relax admission |
| Merge source or standalone-push checkout is not exactly clean | `merge start` / `forge push` | exit 1, `dirty-worktree`; no fetch/rebase/push starts | Includes untracked files and in-progress Git state |
| Selected worktree already has a live merge owner | `merge start` | exit 1, `live-merge-chain-exists`; names chain/status/abort remediation | Ownership claim is atomic |
| Revision-12 full-patch stdout exceeds the otherwise generic 65,536-byte child-output cap while its stream, digest, timeout, process-group, termination, and complete-result proofs remain valid | `merge start` / bootstrap recover | continue the composite child; stream stdout unbounded into SHA-256 and retain only `full_patch_output_digest` | The full-patch stream remains digest-only; patch volume alone is never non-PASS or retained as a transcript |
| Composite bootstrap runs its ordered fetch/full-patch pair, or recovery finds the resulting complete `forge-run-scope-fetch-binding/2` sidecar | `merge start` / bootstrap recover | on PASS or a complete valid surviving sidecar, bind generation 1 and enter `verifying` without rerunning the child; before-sidecar non-PASS retains `classifying` with existing `fetch-failed`; malformed or divergent sidecar is exact `frozen-chain` | Preserve FR-236's sixteen-member sidecar with its three scope members null, its exact two-name publication and digest proof, one child/fence, and no-refetch crash recovery; no scope or name-status constituent exists |
| Commit-family replay cannot authenticate an immutable source fact, or the latest appended record set does not match its exact append state and prefix outside the authenticated `commit_produced` window | commit builder / chain replay | exit 2, `frozen-chain`; no transition | Earlier record sets remain historical and are not currentness-rejected merely because later state exists |
| Authenticated tombstone artifacts are partial or captured state/event facts changed | terminal builder / chain selection | exit 2, `frozen-chain`; no terminal admission | Both artifacts must remain exact captured facts or both must be absent; a malformed or unsafe tombstone never seals a chain |
| Ownership claim is malformed or matches no authoritative ownership event | `merge start` / recover | exit 2, `frozen-chain`; path/inode/digest named; quarantine is offered only after operator-proven repository quiescence/death, halt, complete reconciliation, and proof the inode is garbage unrelated to every acquired owner | Never auto-steal; an acquired owner is not abandonable in Revision 7, and only an unowned-garbage quarantine permits post-move revalidation/halt clear/retry |
| Per-chain lease remains live, foreign, PID-reused/unprovable, malformed, or mismatched for 300 seconds | merge state-changing command | exit 1, `state-precondition`; no state change; a proven-live or unprovable possible owner names only status/wait/establish-quiescence remediation, while quarantine requires operator-proven repository-wide quiescence/death, halt, and event/state reconciliation | Complete FR-232 transition tuple has no admitting row; post-quarantine quiescence is re-proved and the halt explicitly cleared before recovery; only a same-host proven-dead valid lease is auto-reclaimed, and no holder may write without immediately revalidating its canonical lease inode/digest |
| Candidate HEAD changes and existing evidence is bound to its superseded hash | merge command / finalize | exit 1, `candidate-stale`; remote untouched | Exact byte-pinned precondition only; refresh/regenerate before reuse |
| Recorded pre-push worktree path is absent after chain start | merge command / finalize | persist `foreign-git-state`; mutating verb exits 1 exact `state-precondition`; remote untouched | `worktree-missing` remains start-only and `worktree-invalid` remains existing-path-only; never reconstruct or silently rebind |
| Recorded worktree still exists but ceases to be a valid registered source, or checkout/remote/branch/destination ceases to be the fixed target | merge command / finalize | exit 1, exact `worktree-invalid` or `push-target-invalid`; remote untouched | Never silently retarget or rebind evidence |
| Same-OID policy/diff observation, Git metadata, or event/state truth matches no exact recovery/matrix predicate | merge command / recover | persist `foreign-git-state`; mutating verb exits 1 `state-precondition` | Merge does not broaden the v1 `frozen-chain` precondition |
| Initial or integrated merge Gate 1/2 is unavailable or non-PASS | `merge verify` / `merge gate run` / finalize epoch | exit 1, `merge-gate-failed`; `verifying` or `reverification_failed` | Retry only through the state table; remote untouched |
| An epoch plan is omitted, sealed by an event outside the closed sealer set, or its unsealed/sealed members, suite digest/cursor, seal/authorizing event, `gate` fence, or `gate_recorded` result do not identify the same exact mechanical-suite position | merge finalize/recover gate execution | exit 2, exact `frozen-chain`; no later suite child starts | A sealed empty suite is complete; a crash before `gate_recorded` retains the one cursor-selected child; absence of a result never advances the cursor |
| Mandatory portable common-lock arbiter, optional secondary `flock`, portable-owner recovery, recovery reservation, or unresolved in-flight fence exhausts the single 300 s budget, or no portable mechanism exists | `merge start` / `merge refresh` / `merge finalize` / `merge recover` / `merge cleanup` / active-rebase `merge abort` / `forge push` | exit 1, `rebase-lock-unavailable`; no new side effect | Never bypass or select an independent backend; holder/path/inode/digest/mechanism surfaced; a failed fence-publication attempt retries within the same budget and does not immediately use this code |
| A surviving physical fence is encountered before the recovery reservation is published or before reservation-held lifecycle recovery classifies it | common-lock acquisition / recovery | before reservation publication, permit only FR-235's exact read-only identity/death proof needed to construct the immutable reservation; do not classify or clear the fence, complete common-lock admission, or change state; budget exhaustion uses existing `rebase-lock-unavailable` | After publication only the reservation holder may inspect, classify, or clear the fence; ordinary common-lock acquisition never clears it |
| A fenced operation reaches its first publication after the acquisition deadline has elapsed | any FR-236 fenced operation | make the first publication attempt under that operation's fresh bounded 300-second publication budget | The acquisition deadline cannot refuse the first attempt; revalidated retries consume the same non-resetting operation budget, whose exhaustion uses the preceding `rebase-lock-unavailable` row |
| FR-219 commit-lock acquisition fails or times out after standalone push owns the common lock | `forge push` | exit 1, exact `lock-unavailable`; singleton byte-identical and no fetch/rebase/push/recovery write | Release common lock; this is the byte-pinned commit-lock precondition, not a widened common-lock code |
| Strict-valid nonterminal standalone-push singleton exists after commit finalize acquires the commit lock, except the exact repair tuple below | `commit finalize` after phase 4 | exit 1, exact `state-precondition`; commit chain remains `authorized`, candidate/index/HEAD and singleton unchanged | The complete finalize tuple admits ordinary history mutation only after exact next `forge push` recovers/closes the singleton; finalize never waits for the common lock |
| Singleton is exactly strict-valid `recovery_required/activation-policy-invalid/start-new-attempt`, `lock_release` is null, no fence/process is live or unresolved, and the authorized commit candidate is exactly DM-015's canonical `.forge-manifest`-only repair | `commit finalize` after phase 4 | ordinary candidate-bound finalize result; singleton byte-identical | Sole nonterminal interlock exception; every tuple or candidate mismatch takes the preceding `state-precondition` row, and next `forge push` binds the repaired HEAD as a new attempt |
| Locked subprocess times out after 1200 s, exceeds 65,536 output bytes, fails launch, or cannot be proved terminated | `merge start` / `merge refresh` / `merge finalize` / `merge recover` / `merge cleanup` / active-rebase `merge abort` / `forge push` | kill the isolated group and fail closed as `merge-gate-failed`, `fetch-failed`, `rebase-failed`, `push-outcome-unknown`, `cleanup-failed`, or the exact observer-wrapper `state-precondition` by operation; exact `final-prepush` remote observation uses `fetch-failed`, exact `post-push` observation uses `push-outcome-unknown`, and exact `attribution-observation` preserves its wrapper before the same invocation's classification continuation is unadmitted and uses `state-precondition`; merge sets `foreign-git-state` and push sets `recovery_required` on survivor/unknown process truth | Preserve repository-wide fence and its attempt/phase/push-intent/attribution binding until group death is proved; observe Git/remote before retry and never continue to a later side effect |
| Fixed-target fetch/tip resolution, or a final pre-push authoritative tip read fails before push intent — including initial standalone tip resolution that authoritatively finds the required destination absent | `merge start` / `merge refresh` / `merge finalize` / `merge recover` / `forge push` | exit 1, `fetch-failed`; no push | Start/refresh retain `classifying`, merge finalize/recover return to `authorized`, and standalone pre-intent failure records `recovery_required/fetch-failed`; an unavailable observation after push intent instead uses only `push-outcome-unknown` |
| CLI-owned merge or standalone rebase has an exact recoverable conflict | `merge finalize` / `merge recover` / `forge push` | exit 1, `rebase-conflict`; merge state `rebase_conflict` or singleton `recovery_required/rebase-conflict` | Merge names its exact recover argv; standalone names next `forge push`, which may run only the owned abort; remote untouched |
| Explicit `merge recover --continue` or `merge recover --abort-rebase` is invoked outside the selected chain's exact owned `rebase_conflict` tuple | `merge recover` | exit 1, `state-precondition`; diagnostic names the actual scalar state; append no event and make no state change | The flag is never ignored or routed as bare recovery; active-rebase `merge abort` is unchanged |
| Rebase/continue/abort fails outside a recoverable conflict | `merge recover --continue` / `merge recover --abort-rebase` / active-rebase `merge abort` / `forge push` owned rebase recovery | exit 1, `rebase-failed`; remote untouched; merge enters `revising` only on proven restoration and otherwise retains scalar state with `foreign-git-state`; standalone remains `recovery_required` | Logical ownership is not released until restoration is exact; observation only when restoration cannot be proven |
| Standalone attribution observation fails or matches no exact nonce/reflog predicate without an actual Git rebase/abort failure | `forge push` recovery | retain `recovery_required` with `rebase-attribution-unresolved`, then the same invocation's unadmitted classification continuation returns exit 1, `state-precondition`; no push | External same-OID rewrite or contaminated/mismatched reflog is never accepted as Forge output; the exact read-only observer intent survives death/quarantine recovery, standalone operations outside DM-017's seven-member set are `frozen-chain`, and observation remains the only admitted action |
| Integrated generation requires renewed control approval | `merge finalize` | exit 1, `approval-required`; park outside lock | Approval names the exact integrated full HEAD/generation; no TTL |
| Authoritative observation proves the current/latest attempted intended HEAD contained | merge finalize/recover or `forge push` recovery | normalize the active condition to `none` while retaining immutable result evidence; merge records `pushed`, standalone records `closed`, and both bind that current `landed_head`; no new push | This has precedence over every result, retry, rejection, movement, inactivity, and abort row; standalone additionally revalidates locked HEAD/clean truth and cleanup rechecks that exact current head |
| Authoritative observation proves current intended HEAD uncontained but an older attempted head contained | merge finalize/recover or `forge push` recovery | persist latest-contained older `landed_head`, forbid abort/blind retry, and record `remote-moved`; active merge/standalone starts a new bounded integration attempt, while inactive merge uses exact `historical-landed-superseded` tagged `aborted` release | Never report the newer generation pushed/closed or admit cleanup from older-only truth; inactive release retains the clean newer worktree/branch and names a fresh merge start |
| Destination update is rejected as non-fast-forward and authoritative observation shows the destination absent or at an OID differing from the expected old tip with every attempted intended HEAD uncontained | merge finalize/recover or `forge push` | exit 1, `non-fast-forward`; observe before next integration | The unique exact-target porcelain record accepts exactly `[rejected] (non-fast-forward)` or `[rejected] (fetch first)`; never force-push or blindly retry; merge increments the consecutive remote-movement count and the eighth such independently proven defeat uses `remote-churn` |
| Final-prepush or post-push-intent authoritative observation finds the destination absent or moved away from expected old tip with every attempted intended HEAD uncontained and without a retained non-fast-forward rejection | merge finalize/recover or `forge push` | exit 0, `ok: true`, reason `ok`; persist `remote-moved`, no push from that observation | Exact next step starts a new bounded merge epoch or standalone attempt; an absence observed before invocation never starts a push, FR-236's deletion-after-read limitation remains explicit, and the byte-pinned `non-fast-forward` precondition is not broadened |
| A retained known non-non-fast-forward failure is followed by authoritative old-tip observation with every attempted intended HEAD uncontained after that epoch's push attempt | merge finalize/recover or `forge push` | exit 1, `push-failed`; durable next action retries only in a new lock epoch | Before observation this is an internal observation waypoint with no interim envelope; landed, moved/absent, and unobservable observations use their higher-priority rows |
| Push outcome cannot be observed authoritatively | merge recover or `forge push` recovery | exit 1, `push-outcome-unknown`; no retry | Merge retains `pushing`; singleton retains `recovery_required` |
| Proven-not-landed post-attempt abort cannot acquire the common lock or cannot complete its authoritative observation | `merge abort` from `pushing` | exact `rebase-lock-unavailable` for lock acquisition failure or `push-outcome-unknown` for observation failure; remain `pushing`, retain ownership | No all-false cutoff exists, so no ownership-release intent or terminal abort is written |
| Complete rebase lock cannot be explicitly released after primary outcome is durable, including push's inner commit-lock or outer common-lock layer | merge / `forge push` | exit 1, `lock-release-failed`; exact layer identities/order and primary pushed/not-pushed truth preserved | Push retains the portable outer owner while recovery proves/releases inner then outer; recovery never repeats the primary child or side effect. Invalid mode admits any strict-valid nonterminal wrapper only in DM-015 recovery-only mode and stops after identity-bound classification/restoration |
| Pushed containment or non-force worktree/branch cleanup cannot finish | `merge cleanup` | exit 1, `cleanup-failed`; state `cleanup_pending` | Never report not pushed; no force and no second push |
| Standalone-push checkout, remote, branch, or destination is not the fixed sanctioned target | `forge push` | exit 1, `push-target-invalid`; no fetch/rebase/push | Main checkout, `origin`, and configured default branch only |
| Standalone-push singleton is malformed, unreadable, symlinked, or schema-invalid | `forge push` / phase-4 `commit finalize` interlock | exit 2, `frozen-chain`; preserve every existing byte and refuse all mutation | DM-017 expressly defines the singleton as the standalone-push chain file, so this is the exact corrupt-chain-file precondition |
| Remote-only final observation defeats merge epoch 1–7 before push | `merge finalize` / recover | exit 0, `ok: true`, reason `ok`, state `authorized`; exact next step `merge finalize --chain-id <id>` | Safe partial progress: `remote-moved` and carry-forward are durable, no push occurred |
| Eight consecutive epochs are defeated solely by remote movement | `merge finalize` / recover | exit 1, `remote-churn`; park in `awaiting_approval` | Operator acknowledgement required; liveness only under eventual quiescence |
| DM-015 missing or `legacy-v1` | PreToolUse hook | phase-1 commit dual-accept and raw-push behavior unchanged | `forge push` is already available before activation |
| DM-015 `forge-verbs-v1` | PreToolUse hook | raw commit/push deny; sanctioned Forge CLI segments remain legal | Both denials activate atomically |
| DM-015 duplicate, malformed, misplaced, or unknown in the invoking committed HEAD or final intended HEAD | PreToolUse hook / merge finalize / `forge push` | hook denies raw commit/push; post-hook final-HEAD classification internally parks merge in `revising` or standalone in `activation-policy-invalid/start-new-attempt` without a push intent, then the unadmitted push continuation emits the same exact diagnostic and returns exit 1 `state-precondition` without another state change | Only the exact DM-015 manifest-only canonical transform may pass approved commit finalize; a strict-valid nonterminal singleton admits only identity-bound recovery/death proof/owned restoration/observation/release with no new integration or push, then stops at valid restoration, landed truth, the repairable activation tuple, or its ordinary unresolved result |
| Activated raw `git commit` | PreToolUse hook | deny, `forge: raw git commit denied — use Forge CLI commit finalize` | Narrow raw-verb PreToolUse claim only |
| Activated raw `git push` | PreToolUse hook | deny, `forge: raw git push denied — use Forge CLI merge finalize or Forge CLI push` | An allowed CLI segment never blesses an appended raw segment |
| Duplicate `--repo`, `--run-id`, or `--chain-id` | `cli.py` / typed-builder singleton parser | refusal, `forge: CLI option refused — duplicate <option>`; `option-duplicate` for `forge-cli/2` | Reject before repository or chain selection; never duplicate-last |
| Empty required singleton option | `cli.py` / typed-builder singleton parser | refusal, `forge: CLI option refused — empty <option>`; `option-empty` for `forge-cli/2` | Reject before repository or chain selection |
| Caller supplies a `run_id` outside the FR-250 grammar (empty, dot-prefixed, or containing a path separator, NUL or control character) | `run-open` / journal append | exit 1, `forge: <operation> refused — invalid run id`; no directory or file created | Malformed-envelope class under FR-251; the rejected value is never echoed |
| Journal append encounters an I/O failure | journal writer | exit 1, `forge: journal append failed: I/O error` | The primary execution or gate result is unchanged; no policy refusal is introduced. |
| Proposed record is not a JSON object or lacks `kind` or `run_id` | journal writer | exit 1, `forge: journal append refused: record must be a JSON object with kind and run_id` | Unknown and legacy kinds and optional references are accepted without semantic validation. |
| A journal line is not a parseable JSON object | `validate` | exit 1, `forge: journal validate failed: malformed JSON object at line <n>` | Structural failure only; never a gate or lifecycle decision. |
| `commit restage --paths` names a path that is not a safe repository path (outside the worktree, under `.git`, or resolving through a symlink to a location outside the worktree) | `commit restage` | exit 1, `path-missing`, `forge: commit restage refused — unsafe path: <path>`; nothing staged | FR-255; `<path>` is an escaped single-line JSON string literal for a syntactically valid pathspec and the literal `(un-echoed)` otherwise; the candidate and every evidence record are unchanged |
| Review requested at the eight-round cap, counting rounds inherited from a predecessor chain | `review request` | exit 1, `iteration-cap`, `forge: review refused — iteration cap reached: <rounds> rounds (<inherited> inherited from <predecessor-chain-id>)` | FR-255; only `commit skip review-cap --reason <text>` before the first review request resets an inherited budget |
| `commit start` or `merge start` is given the retired run-binding options `--run-id` or `--task` | `commit start` / `merge start` | exit 1, `state-precondition`, `forge: <verb> refused — --run-id and --task are not admitted`; no chain created | FR-256; the options stay global for `launch` only; every other retired `cli.py` flag refuses through the argument parser's existing invalid-invocation diagnostic, and retired `codex_orch_tools.py` flags refuse with argparse's usage error, exit 2 |
| `review request` for `review-final` resolves to the same provider and model as the implementer route | `review request` | exit 1, `state-precondition`, `forge: review request refused — review-final route equals the implementer route`; no request created | FR-032; the committed defaults (Codex implementer, Claude `review-final`) satisfy the rule; `review-cheap` requires only a distinct agent |
| `commit skip review-cap` issued after the chain's first review request | `commit skip` | exit 1, `state-precondition`, `forge: commit skip refused — review-cap is admitted only before the first review request`; no record written | FR-255; the inherited base is unchanged |

---

## Behavioral Scenarios

### Scenario: Structural validation preserves legacy and unknown records

**Traces to**: FR-250..FR-252, FR-256
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** a run journal containing parseable legacy records, unknown kinds, and an activated binding
- **When** the new reader and structural `validate` run
- **Then** every record remains readable without migration, binding replay, gate correlation, or lifecycle validation
- **And** neither a legacy activation sidecar nor an unresolved reference can prevent a new structurally valid append

### Scenario: Commit passes the full chain and the guard admits it

**Traces to**: FR-050, FR-052, FR-054, FR-055, FR-090
**Category**: Happy Path

- **Given** a forge-initialized repo with filled regions and staged changes in the `python` category only
- **When** `/forge:commit` stages the target paths, snapshots the index tree, and review-cheap returns PASS on iteration 1 over the immutable tree-to-tree review artifact
- **Then** the stack validations for `python` are executed, the exact four-line marker `.forge/tmp/authorized/<authorization-id>` names the reviewed candidate tree, prepare validates the live inherited PID and re-observes that tree under the persistent lock, the next tool call contains only `git commit --cleanup=verbatim -m <safely shell-quoted literal>`, Git succeeds, cleanup verifies its exact parent/tree/message through bounded raw-object reading, then releases the lock and deletes only that marker

### Scenario: Commit Step 5 cleans up across denial, failure, and interruption

**Traces to**: FR-050, FR-054, FR-055, FR-090, FR-221, DM-010
**Category**: Error Path

- **Given** an authorized v2 marker, an unchanged index tree, and one live harness-provided `FORGE_SESSION_PID` inherited by fresh shells
- **When** prepare succeeds and the standalone commit call is denied by the hook, fails in Git, or is interrupted before a Git result
- **Then** the separate cleanup call still attempts both PID-owned lock release and deletion of only the explicit candidate marker before another attempt
- **And** unsafe bundled, substituted, or option-bearing commit shapes retain the exact marker-hash-mismatch public denial
- **When** Git succeeds but cleanup fails or is interrupted
- **Then** the skill reports commit success together with cleanup failure, resumes cleanup without retrying the successful commit, and leaves every unrelated marker untouched

### Scenario: Direct commit without the gate chain is blocked

**Traces to**: FR-090
**Category**: Error Path

- **Given** a forge-initialized repo with staged changes and no matching `.forge/tmp/authorized/<authorization-id>`
- **When** any agent runs `git commit -m "quick fix"` via Bash
- **Then** the PreToolUse guard denies with reason `forge: commit not authorized — run /forge:commit (marker missing)`

### Scenario: Operator halt blocks reintegration everywhere

**Traces to**: FR-090, FR-091, FR-092
**Category**: Error Path

- **Given** the operator has created `AGENT_HALT` at the main checkout root
- **When** `/forge:worktree-merge` reaches its pre-rebase halt check
- **Then** the merge stops before acquiring the rebase lock with `operator halt engaged — not merging`
- **And** a `halt detected` line is appended to `.forge/tmp/halt-audit.log`
- **But** the sentinel file is not deleted by any agent

### Scenario: Marker gone stale after 30 minutes

**Traces to**: FR-054, FR-090
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** a v2 marker whose tree and authorization ID match the live index but whose `authorized-at` is 31 minutes old
- **When** `git commit` is attempted
- **Then** the guard denies with exact reason `forge: commit not authorized — run /forge:commit (marker stale)`

### Scenario: Assertion sensor blocks only the Python AST branch and honors a per-file waiver

**Traces to**: FR-139, FR-144
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** a touched Python test function has no recognized assertion and no waiver
- **When** commit Step 2 runs `check-test-quality.py`
- **Then** it prints `forge: assertion-free test detected: <path>:<line>:<test-name>`, exits 1, and blocks the commit
- **When** that same test file instead contains `# forge-assertion-waiver: generated oracle is checked by the integration harness`
- **Then** the sensor does not block for that file, but carries that exact path and reason into commit and review evidence
- **And** the waiver does not skip the test command, mutation, invariants, or sensing in any other file

### Scenario: A stack with no seeded assertion heuristic is advisory

**Traces to**: FR-139, FR-144
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** a touched non-Python test belongs to a stack whose seed declares `No seeded assertion heuristic for <stack>.`
- **When** commit Step 2 runs `check-test-quality.py`
- **Then** it prints `forge: no seeded assertion heuristic for <stack> — advisory only`, exits 0, and includes the absence in review evidence
- **But** the absence of a rule never blocks the commit

### Scenario: Fresh repo initialized fail-closed, then filled

**Traces to**: FR-037, FR-050, FR-061, FR-070, FR-071, FR-080, FR-082, FR-083, FR-090, FR-149
**Category**: Happy Path

- **Given** a git repo with a Python test suite in CI and no forge files
- **And** an uncommitted candidate policy command would create a sentinel if executed
- **When** `/forge:init` completes region filling and the operator approves the hard bootstrap diff
- **Then** the candidate command has not run, review-final saw the exact immutable snapshot patch under the fixed bootstrap context, and the exact four-line v2 reviewed marker admits only its authorization tree
- **And** the first commit contains the filled policy plus `.forge-manifest` with `init_completed: false`
- **When** init resumes from that clean HEAD
- **Then** Gate 1 and stack validations run from the committed policy, the sentinel behavior is now observable, and the activation diff setting `init_completed: true` receives a fresh control review and explicit approval before its second commit
- **And** AGENTS.md contains the content between `<!-- FORGE:BEGIN -->`/`<!-- FORGE:END -->`, CLAUDE.md contains `@forge-project.md`, and the gitignore block appears once

### Scenario: Merge attempted before init fills the gate command

**Traces to**: FR-061
**Category**: Error Path

- **Given** a repo where `forge-project.md` still carries the `forge-init:` sentinel inside `gate1-test-command`
- **When** `/forge:worktree-merge` reaches Gate 1
- **Then** the merge fails with `forge: gate1-test-command not configured — run /forge:init` and exit 1, and the worktree is left intact

### Scenario: Re-init preserves the operator's filled regions

**Traces to**: FR-072, FR-084
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** a forge-initialized repo whose `gate1-test-command` region is filled, whose filled `risk-tiers` region has operator rows around one valid but older fixed dependency block, whose current-format `reviewer-facing-eval-triggers` body contains a well-formed narrowed path, and whose `changelog-policy` region still carries its `forge-init:` comment
- **When** `/forge:init` runs again
- **Then** the `gate1-test-command` body and every `risk-tiers` byte outside the dependency delimiters are byte-identical after re-init, the delimited dependency block, complete canonical reviewer-trigger region, and unfilled region are refreshed from the current template, and no eval fixture or `.result` baseline is overwritten

### Scenario: Re-init preserves project-owned spine and Codex content

**Traces to**: DM-003, FR-038, FR-072, FR-080, FR-084
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** a forge-initialized repo with project spine addenda, a foreign Codex hook, and foreign `config.toml` content. (Revision 20 authority; deferred to task G42 of run-20261003-archive)
- **When** `/forge:init` preflights every Codex input and applies a newer template. (Revision 20 authority; deferred to task G42 of run-20261003-archive)
- **Then** the addenda, original install date, and foreign hook survive byte-for-byte; fresh marker-owned hooks replace stale ones; the existing config is retained beside an idempotent `.forge-new` collision; and malformed, oversized, or unsafe inputs refuse before any target mutation. (Revision 20 authority; deferred to task G42 of run-20261003-archive)

### Scenario: Implementer launch ordering and detachment

**Traces to**: FR-034, FR-035, FR-036, FR-037
**Category**: Happy Path

- **Given** an active task with an assigned worktree
- **When** the orchestrate skill launches a fresh implementer execution
- **Then** `prompt.md` and an empty `events.jsonl` exist before the journal `execution_started` append is attempted; a failed append is reported and does not block the process launch, the command uses `--json --output-last-message` with literal absolute redirect paths and no `--ephemeral`, and the process runs detached (`nohup`/`disown`)

### Scenario: Reviewer isolation on first-pass review

**Traces to**: FR-032
**Category**: Happy Path

- **Given** a completed implementer execution with a handoff claiming all tests pass
- **When** the orchestrator launches the Codex first-pass review
- **Then** the reviewer runs as a fresh agent with `-s read-only`, and its `prompt.md` contains the target SHA and acceptance criteria
- **But** contains no implementer handoff text, claimed test results, or prior verdicts

### Scenario: Final review BLOCK accepts the fix in the same chain

**Traces to**: FR-053, FR-065, FR-121, FR-255
**Category**: Error Path

- **Given** review-final returns BLOCK with one MAJOR finding on review round 1
- **When** a fix changes the candidate's file set and `commit restage` records it in that chain
- **Then** Gate 1, candidate-bound mechanical cells and triggered fresh-reviewer evals rerun, affected conditional gates rerun, and any carried result has the deterministic delta proof required by FR-255
- **And** a fresh review round sees the prior candidate identity and exact delta while its verdict binds the new complete candidate
- **And** prior approval is void, the review count advances within the same cap, and applicable e2e verification still follows FR-121

### Scenario: Review hits the 8-iteration cap

**Traces to**: FR-053
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** a review loop whose 8th iteration returns BLOCK
- **When** the cap is reached
- **Then** the residual risk (outstanding findings and why they remain) is recorded, the user is escalated to, and no commit or merge occurs
- **And** a chain started afterwards in the same worktree or branch inherits the effective round count, including through a predecessor that landed a fast-tier candidate without review, so neither abort, restart, nor an unreviewed landing resets the cap; only `commit skip review-cap --reason <text>` by the operator before the new chain's first review request does

### Scenario: Reviewer confirmation round resumes the reviewer session

**Traces to**: FR-033
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** a completed review execution `codex-review-01/execution-01` with recorded `session_id`
- **When** the orchestrator requests a targeted recheck of one fix
- **Then** the launch uses `codex exec ... resume <session_id> -` into directory `codex-review-01/execution-02`
- **But** the command contains no `-C` flag

### Scenario: Stale monitor notification treated as ambiguous

**Traces to**: FR-040, FR-041
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** a `codex_agent_stale` notification after a laptop sleep, while the launched process group is still alive
- **When** the orchestrator applies the staleness protocol
- **Then** it checks events-file mtime and process liveness and re-arms the monitor
- **But** appends no `execution_finished` based on staleness alone

### Scenario: Locked rebase reintegration with post-rebase re-verify

**Traces to**: FR-060, FR-062, FR-063, FR-064
**Category**: Happy Path

- **Given** Gates 1–3 passed in a worktree while the default branch advanced by one commit
- **When** the merge step runs under the rebase lock
- **Then** the branch is rebased onto `origin/<default-branch>`, Gates 1 and 2 are re-run against the integrated tip inside the lock and pass, the branch is then fast-forward pushed, and the worktree and branch are removed only after the push succeeds — the worktree remove uses no `--force`

### Scenario: flock missing on stock macOS

**Traces to**: FR-062 (Revision-13 portable-arbiter cutover amendment), FR-235
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** a stock macOS host where `command -v flock` fails
- **When** `/forge:worktree-merge` acquires the lock through `common-lock hold --owner-kind push --operation push`
- **Then** the wrapper holds the mandatory portable arbiter (owner record plus hard-linked `agent-rebase.lockdir`) and still takes the optional kernel layer on `agent-rebase.lock` through Python's `fcntl.flock`, because the `flock` binary is irrelevant to the wrapper (the layer is omitted only where the interpreter lacks `fcntl.flock`); the skill treats the `forge-common-lock-ready/1` record as acquisition, the rebase is serialized against every Linux and macOS entrant on the same inode namespace within the wrapper's 300 s deadline, and the skill creates or removes no lock artifact of its own

**Traces to**: FR-055, DM-010

- **Then** lock acquisition and release observe the same owner PID, and no skill exports `$$`, `$PPID`, or a tool-process PID
- **When** the inherited PID is syntactically valid but dead or unverifiable
- **Then** the operation refuses exactly `forge: FORGE_SESSION_PID does not name a live same-host session owner` before any lock or marker byte changes

### Scenario: Concurrent decision-event appends survive pruning without an append lock

**Traces to**: FR-005, FR-157
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** concurrent emitters and `drift-check.sh` operate on `events.jsonl` on a supported local POSIX filesystem
- **When** each emitter registers in flight, makes one checked `os.write()` through an `O_APPEND` descriptor without acquiring `.forge/tmp/events.lock`, and a prune overlaps by holding that lock across writer drain, read, and atomic replace
- **Then** every complete event line not selected for retention removal survives exactly once and no append is interleaved, duplicated, or lost through the emit/prune race
- **And** an emitter that observes a live prune polls below one second for at most 5 seconds, then records `event-append-lock-timeout` and leaves its already-delivered primary outcome and exit status unchanged

### Scenario: CLI commit chain passes end to end and the hook admits the finalize commit

**Traces to**: FR-210, FR-211, FR-214, FR-215, FR-219, FR-221
**Category**: Happy Path

- **Given** a clean worktree, no live chain, and a non-control change on explicit paths
- **When** `commit start` stages and classifies, `verify` runs every mechanical step to PASS, the review verb for the derived tier returns PASS, and `finalize --message` runs
- **Then** the chain traverses `classifying → verifying → reviewing → authorized → committing → closed`, authorization records bind the tree authorization ID while review records separately bind the exact patch evidence, the produced identity PASS precedes consumption and landing, the lock is held only across finalize, and the hook admits through the chain side of dual-accept

**Traces to**: FR-214

**Traces to**: FR-219

### Scenario: A commit hook changes the candidate and finalize freezes

### Scenario: CLI merge completes from clean worktree through cleanup

- **Then** the chain traverses `classifying → verifying → reviewing → authorized → rebasing → pushing → pushed → closed`

### Scenario: CLI control merge waits for exact-HEAD approval

### Scenario: Conflict-free HEAD rewrite still forces fresh judgment

**Traces to**: FR-234, FR-235
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** a reviewed merge whose rebase rewrites the candidate full HEAD while preserving the diff digest
- **When** integrated mechanical checks pass
- **Then** the chain parks in `reviewing`, releases the common lock, and requires fresh Gate 3 and Gate 4
- **And** a control merge requires new approval naming the rewritten integrated HEAD

### Scenario: Rebase conflicts recover only through the authorized path set

**Traces to**: FR-232, FR-236

### Scenario: Integrated diff or policy change invalidates all judgment

**Traces to**: FR-233..FR-235
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** a reviewed generation enters a locked integration epoch
- **When** the exact diff digest or committed policy digest changes
- **Then** the CLI reclassifies, repeats Gates 1–4, and renews control approval
- **And** no remote-only carry-forward record is permitted

### Scenario: Final in-lock gate failure leaves the remote untouched

**Traces to**: FR-232, FR-233, FR-235
**Category**: Error

- **Given** an integrated generation in `reverifying`
- **When** Gate 1 or Gate 2 returns non-PASS or mutates the bound tuple
- **Then** the chain enters `reverification_failed` or reclassification, returns `merge-gate-failed`, releases the lock, and performs no push
- **And** bare recovery may retry the complete integrated pair only while the tuple remains unchanged

### Scenario: Push race and unknown outcome are never blindly retried

**Traces to**: FR-232, FR-235, FR-236
**Category**: Error

- **Given** push intent durably records old tip O, intended HEAD H, and destination D
- **When** a race rejects the update with the unique target's exact porcelain `[rejected] (non-fast-forward)` or `[rejected] (fetch first)` record and authoritative observation shows D moved elsewhere without containing H
- **Then** the chain records `non-fast-forward`, increments the consecutive remote-movement count, and returns to integration rather than force-pushing; the eighth such remote-only defeat records `remote-churn` and parks for acknowledgement
- **When** a successful authoritative query instead proves D absent
- **Then** the observation records `exists: false`, starts no push from that observation, and selects `non-fast-forward` only for a retained actual rejection or otherwise `remote-moved`
- **But** if D is deleted only after the final successful read, the exact ordinary non-force refspec may recreate it; post-attempt observation establishes landed or moved truth and the spec does not misclassify that race as a guaranteed non-fast-forward rejection
- **When** D cannot be observed authoritatively
- **Then** it remains `pushing`, emits `push-outcome-unknown`, and no retry occurs until observation succeeds

### Scenario: Standalone push serializes and accepts an already-integrated no-op

**Traces to**: DM-017, FR-235, FR-238
**Category**: Happy Path

- **Given** the main checkout is clean, the fetched default-branch tip is already an ancestor of its unchanged HEAD, and no reflog entry bears the new standalone operation prefix
- **When** two `forge push` invocations contend
- **Then** neither writes the singleton until it owns the complete common lock, the winner re-reads or creates the exact DM-017 record, and the loser observes that durable record only after acquiring the lock
- **And** the winner classifies the rebase as successful no-op integration, records `rebased_head = pre_operation_head`, persists push intent, and performs at most one exact ordinary non-force refspec after proving ancestry to the observed old tip
- **But** an unrelated destination movement observed before an update records `remote-moved` and returns safe partial success; only an actual ordinary-update rejection may return `non-fast-forward`
- **And** a nonterminal singleton left by a crashed winner makes later `commit finalize` unadmitted under its commit lock until `forge push` recovers it
- **And** failure to release push's inner commit-lock or outer common-lock layer records one ordered `lock-release-failed` transaction that retains primary truth and releases inner before outer without replay

### Scenario: Historical attempted-head containment prevents false retry or abort

**Traces to**: DM-014, DM-017, FR-232, FR-236, FR-238
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** one transaction durably attempted H1, parked after remote movement, then integrated and attempted a different H2 without erasing H1 or either result
- **When** one fresh authoritative observation proves the destination contains H1 but not H2
- **Then** full-vector priority selects H1 as partial `landed_head`, forbids abort/blind retry, and parks active merge at `authorized/remote-moved` or standalone at `recovery_required/remote-moved` for a fresh H2 integration attempt; neither reports H2 pushed/closed or admits cleanup
- **And** that fresh attempt is identified by its durable new epoch/rebase intent even if no-op integration yields H2 again; prior H1/H2 containment cannot re-park it before intent, an unchanged final tip appends exactly one H3 or deliberate repeated-H2 member and permits its one push, while moved/unavailable truth takes only the ordinary pre-intent row
- **And** if the merge chain is inactive, it instead binds the exact `historical-landed-superseded` release cutoff, retains the clean H2 worktree/branch without deletion, releases ownership, records tagged `aborted`, and names a fresh `merge start --worktree` as the next step

### Scenario: Crash after rebase or push is resolved by observation

**Traces to**: DM-014, DM-017, FR-236, FR-238
**Category**: Error

- **Given** intent was fsynced before a rebase or push and the process crashed before materialized close
- **When** recovery observes an owned merge integrated HEAD with the chain nonce/reflog proof, it resumes at the matrix-selected re-verification state
- **And** a standalone-push completed rebase advances only when its DM-017 nonce, exact action prefix, main-checkout identity, clean/ancestry facts, and contiguous newest reflog segment all match
- **And** when a freshly fetched destination contains the current intended HEAD, it records `pushed`/`closed` without pushing again; older-only containment instead records partial `landed_head` and takes the exact active reconciliation or inactive tagged-terminal row without discarding the newer unlanded work
- **But** any state matching neither the recorded before-state nor a provable result sets merge `foreign-git-state` or leaves standalone push `recovery_required`
- **And** an unprovably dead child leaves the common-dir in-flight fence, so a second merge chain and standalone push both receive `rebase-lock-unavailable` even after the physical lock holder exits

### Scenario: Cleanup retry preserves pushed truth

**Traces to**: FR-232, FR-236
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** the remote contains the intended HEAD but non-force worktree removal or branch deletion failed
- **When** the chain enters `cleanup_pending` and `merge cleanup` is retried
- **Then** it tolerates an already absent worktree or branch, resumes only incomplete cleanup, and closes after containment succeeds
- **And** it never reports not pushed, force-removes content, or runs push again

### Scenario: Activation truth table is fail-closed and atomic

**Traces to**: DM-015, FR-239, FR-240
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** committed mode is missing or `legacy-v1`
- **Then** phase-1 marker-or-chain commit admission and raw-push behavior remain byte-identical while `forge push` is legal
- **Given** committed mode is `forge-verbs-v1`
- **Then** raw commit and raw push are both denied atomically while all three sanctioned CLI producer paths remain legal
- **Given** the mode is duplicate, malformed, or unknown
- **Then** raw mutation receives the exact invalid-mode denial and an approved `forge commit finalize` can land a repair
- **And** if standalone push parked the strict-valid singleton at exact `activation-policy-invalid/start-new-attempt`, that canonical repair-only finalize is the sole nonterminal interlock exception, preserves the singleton byte-for-byte, and every adjacent candidate or singleton tuple remains blocked
- **And** if rebase already produced invalid committed HEAD but crashed before classification, during conflict/process recovery, or during ordered release, invalid mode admits `forge push` only in identity-bound recovery-only mode for death proof, observation/classification, exact owned restoration, and release; it starts no integration/push work and stops at valid restoration, landed truth, the repairable tuple, or its exact unresolved result
- **When** the outer hook saw valid mode but rebase produces an intended HEAD with invalid DM-015
- **Then** the independently tested final parse emits the same exact diagnostic, returns `state-precondition`, persists no push intent, and requires repaired-head refresh or a fresh standalone attempt

### Scenario: Standalone push deliberately performs no chain provenance audit

**Traces to**: FR-238, FR-239
**Category**: Happy Path

- **Given** the main checkout is clean on the configured default branch and contains a legitimate commit that no closed merge chain produced
- **When** `forge push` passes halt, fixed-target, locked fetch/rebase, and fast-forward checks
- **Then** it may push that commit and records the observation in `forge-push/1`
- **And** neither the hook nor CLI invents a chain-produced-SHA requirement

---

### Scenario: Worktree cleanup preserves branch and landing proofs

**Traces to**: FR-064, FR-236
**Category**: Error Path

- **Given** a landed worktree whose branch tip, attachment, matching HEAD, cleanliness, or containment cannot be proved
- **When** automated cleanup runs
- **Then** it preserves the worktree and branch until the applicable proofs pass
- **And** branch deletion follows fresh post-removal tip and containment checks and uses `git update-ref -d` with the verified old oid, never `git branch -D`
- **And** a journal, run-close record, or missing archive supplies no cleanup precondition

### Scenario: Journal close records an outcome without judging the work

**Traces to**: FR-250..FR-252
**Category**: Happy Path

- **Given** a run has unfinished tasks, a BLOCK verdict reference, and no recorded gate results
- **When** the writer appends `run_closed` with a free-text outcome
- **Then** it succeeds without a judgment, validation payload, owner check, scope check, route check, or chain-state check
- **And** a missing or malformed journal cannot authorize or deny any chain verb, approval, landing, or merge

### Scenario: Execution records describe the route actually used

**Traces to**: FR-244..FR-246, FR-250..FR-252
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** reviewer configuration changes between two executions in the same run
- **When** the second execution starts and finishes
- **Then** its records describe its actual role, provider, model, and effort without reopening the run or comparing a frozen opening route
- **And** independent reviewer routing, reviewer distinctness under FR-032, and route-configuration validation remain enforced at launch and review

### Scenario: Gate engine change requires final review without control approval

**Traces to**: FR-051, FR-151, FR-153, FR-253..FR-254
**Category**: Happy Path

- **Given** a candidate touches only `scripts/forge/forge_cli/engine/_core.py` and ordinary changelog text under the shipped policy
- **When** commit and merge classify the immutable candidate
- **Then** the built-in `review-final-floor` makes it hard, records its matched floor rows in classification evidence, and requires review-final
- **And** the engine path alone requires no control approval
- **When** a consumer retains `scripts/**` in its project control row
- **Then** that extension is honoured and the same change also requires explicit operator approval

### Scenario: Operator resets an inherited review budget before the first review request

**Traces to**: FR-255, FR-217, DM-012
**Category**: Edge

- **Given** chain A in a worktree reached three review rounds and was aborted, and chain B started in the same worktree with `inherited_rounds: 3` and `predecessor_chain_id` naming A
- **When** the operator runs `commit skip review-cap --reason <text>` on B before B's first `review request`
- **Then** B records a `user_skip` under step key `review-cap`, emits the `user_skip` decision event with `gate: review-cap`, and its effective inherited base is zero for every cap decision, fresh-eval `iteration` numbering and any successor's inheritance, while `inherited_rounds` and `predecessor_chain_id` stay as history
- **And** the same verb after B's first review request refuses with `state-precondition` and exactly `forge: commit skip refused — review-cap is admitted only before the first review request`, a model-path issuance is denied by the FR-221 hook, and B keeps its inherited base

### Scenario: Restaging cannot revive superseded approval or a failed gate

**Traces to**: FR-053, FR-255
**Category**: Error Path

- **Given** a chain has reviewed candidate A, then candidate B, and restages A again
- **When** the new candidate is evaluated
- **Then** Gate 1, candidate-bound mechanical cells, triggered fresh-reviewer evals, and review rerun, approval is void, and conditional reuse requires a recorded proof against the immediately superseded candidate
- **And** no missing, failed, skipped, stale, or unproved result is silently treated as PASS, and a missing dependency declaration covers every repository path
- **And** an eighth BLOCK escalates with residual risk and permits no landing or reset through another chain for the same unresolved review

### Scenario: Drift ignores run records and retired archive inputs

**Traces to**: FR-160..FR-166
**Category**: Edge Case

- **Given** two clean repository snapshots with identical eligible gate output, chain evidence, committed policy/text and decision telemetry
- **When** they differ only in excluded run-journal, run-archive or learn artefacts
- **Then** those artefacts supply no drift input or semantic finding; report metadata may identify the different snapshots
- **And** an uncommitted tracked archive edit still triggers the ordinary dirty-worktree refusal
- **And** staleness nudges and the operator-cleared CRITICAL workflow block retain their existing enforcement

## 11. Testing Requirements

### Unit

- Journal contract: every minimal kind; complete append-only JSON lines under the append lock; only malformed-envelope and I/O refusals; unknown and every legacy kind carried through; structural validation independent of references, lifecycle, ownership, scope, and route; no migration or sidecar activation; existing bytes unchanged.
- Fix-in-chain: candidate file additions, deletions, renames, and arbitrary in-repository replacement paths after BLOCK; deterministic delta and gate-trigger matching; Gate 1, candidate-bound mechanical cells, triggered fresh-reviewer evals, and review always rerun; other conditional gates carry only a proven unaffected PASS, with complete old/new committed dependency coverage and absent declarations covering all repository paths; new applicability and changed command/policy/tool/profile/input identities force reruns; prior-candidate identity and full delta in review context; A-B-A cannot revive approval; existing review/fresh-eval/merge-attempt accounting survives restages and new chains for the same unresolved review; mandatory merge in-lock gates remain fresh; every controlling predicate has a focused in-memory disable proof.
- Drift detachment: journal, archive, gotcha, and learning-candidate changes cannot affect the input set; gate output, chain evidence, committed text, telemetry, staleness, and CRITICAL workflow enforcement remain operative.

- Commit-guard decision logic: halt; byte-identical denial literals `marker missing`, `marker malformed`, `marker stale`, `marker hash mismatch`; exact four/five/six-line v2 DM-006 forms and rejected permutations; strict LF, format, filename/ID/tree/OID-length, TTL, skip/fast exclusivity, and legacy non-admission; content-addressed common-root lookup from each invoking Git index, same-tree authorization, PASS-only candidate deletion, mismatch retention, marker collision isolation, and stale sweep preserving the current candidate's `marker stale` result; the exact FR-090 manifest predicate; non-forge passthrough; command-form matrix; fast policy checks and independent immutable candidate eligibility; first-policy bootstrap admission only for the exact reviewed tree
- `check-halt.sh` scoped/global sentinels, audit-line format, worktree-transparent root resolution
- Lock scripts: explicit lock-path acquisition/release; omitted-argument `.forge/tmp/commit-lock` default with byte-for-byte behavior/output parity; stale-PID takeover; timeout; foreign-owner release refusal; explicit `.forge/tmp/events.lock` prune acquisition/release; and recovery-only stale prune-lock revalidation that never acquires the lock for an emitter
- Revision-8 legacy commit Step 5 with candidate-v2 amendment: live inherited PID refusal before mutation; prepare failure-only cleanup and disarm boundary; persistent lock ownership across three fresh tool calls; standalone literal `git commit --cleanup=verbatim -m <safely shell-quoted literal>` acceptance; former bundled command, preceding command, command/process substitution, and unsafe-option negatives retaining the byte-identical marker-hash-mismatch denial; shared marker parsing/index-tree observation and bounded raw produced-object verification; release plus candidate-only marker deletion after verified success or unchanged-HEAD ordinary failure; marker retention and no success events after produced mismatch; success, halt, index drift, hook denial, Git failure, interruption before/after each boundary, partial cleanup failure, successful-commit/no-retry behavior, and proof that worktree-merge retains one composite file-descriptor-lock invocation

- Test quality/mutation: every `stacks.md` stack has the FR-139 assertion/mutation/property triple or exact absence; Python AST assertion/raise/assertion-call/expected-exception and blocking cases; valid/invalid per-file waiver scope and evidence; seeded non-Python advisory findings; no-seeded-heuristic advisory pass; malformed-path exit 2; merge trigger truth table (test touched/source added); scoped argv; positive-base-10 timeout-column parsing, explicit nonnumeric/suffixed/non-positive rejection, and legacy 600-second default; process-group kill on mutation timeout; malformed mutation region exact diagnostic/skip/nonblocking disposition; advisory evidence reaches review without journal persistence; full-suite drift-only
- Invariant parsing/execution: three enforcement values, empty/malformed rows and exact malformed-policy diagnostic, committed-HEAD versus working-tree injection, argv/no-`eval`, process-group timeout kill, 65,536-byte cap, fail-closed gate results, advisory 2-second hook budget, and outside-repo inertness
- Risk tiers: pathspec matching/overlap; `trigger-paths` only for the glob floor; absent/filled-empty trigger region contributes no globs; prose `project-triggers` isolation;
  malformed nonempty trigger row hard; non-narrowable control and review-final floors; exact matched-floor evidence; project extensions and no table-based narrowing; engine-code final review without approval unless project control extends to it; unmatched standard; exact shared committed dependency block and unknown-stack standard; category opt-in for `@formatting-only`; trailing-whitespace/line-ending positives; Python de-indent, YAML nesting, every exclusion-floor category, leading/interior whitespace, add/delete/rename/type/binary negatives; declared-tier promotion/no demotion; classifier/guard predicate parity; marker policy ancestor-or-equal; and policy-byte drift
- Decision telemetry: every FR-157 emitter and exact six-key/thirteen-event JSONL grammar; one-line append; assertion blocking/advisory/waived mapping; one occurrence per review-cheap/review-final finding with stable severity reason;
