- Migration: manifest classifier truth table at FR-080's existing step; actual-location byte-identical salvage; all-marker orphan derivation with source path, marker name, and complete body byte preservation; divergent-copy operator selection; fixture and committed-baseline byte identity, import-before-seed, gap-only seed, no-remint, and STRICT ordering; reviewer-shadow activation block; upstream content signatures and `.pre-migration` backups; deregistration without TOML deletion; content-based gitignore reconciliation and both postconditions; disk-derived collision-free migration report with all required legacy/lock facts
- Concurrency: commit-marker isolation, one live commit chain per worktree, atomic merge ownership, lock contention, and append-only session telemetry with the exact 23-column grammar, valid single header, noninterleaved complete invocation blocks, and no lost row or changed prior byte; concurrent journal appends preserve complete lines without scope admission or session ownership.
- Forge CLI commit chain: out-of-order refusal at every FR-211 transition edge; stale-candidate refusal; authorization TTL expiry at 30 minutes from issuance (not chain age); consumed-token refusal; cross-chain authorization isolation; restage and out-of-band index-change invalidation including classification rerun; control-class chains cannot reach `authorized` without an approval record naming the current candidate; one-live-chain-per-worktree refusal; dirty-index refusal with paths named; tree-vs-index drift refusal at review request and finalize; iteration-cap refusal and escalation at 8; above-MINOR disposition requiring the operator mechanism; `head_moved` detection at every verb with the old→new diagnostic and graded `commit rebase` disposition (unchanged candidate keeps the review verdict, gates always re-run, changed policy bytes end the chain); `verify` resumability and passed-verify no-op; mutating-gate precedence refusal; DM-013 fingerprint mismatch voiding the gate-1 pair; per-tier structure (a fast chain that skipped anything but the reviewer fails, including the FR-144/FR-147 rows); the complete add/change/delete/pre-existing gitlink matrix under repository and inherited suppression configuration; bounded raw commit-object rejection for malformed headers, OIDs, identity, and oversize data; real pre-commit-hook produced-object mutants; finalize two-phase crash-window recovery per FR-219 at every boundary from raw identity artifact creation through identity evidence, authorization consumption, landing, and close, with every non-recovery verb refusing in `committing`; every internal finalize check independently disableable with a focused failing test; `--json` stdout parsing as exactly one envelope; and output-contract conformance — every refusal carries a known reason code, remediation, and the `next required step:` line

### E2E Smoke

- Full run on a fixture repo with `fake_codex.py`: two-commit init bootstrap/activation → workflow with implementer and independent reviews → candidate-bound gated commit and merge → free-text `run_closed` → report; legacy and unknown JSON record kinds remain readable.
- Revision-8 legacy commit smoke with candidate-v2 amendment: marker creation uses the shared tree snapshot and exact four/five/six-line renderer; prepare parses and re-observes through that helper under the persistent lock; the next Bash call is only `git commit --cleanup=verbatim -m <literal>`; cleanup reads the bounded raw commit object, requires exact parent/tree/message identity, releases the lock, deletes only after PASS or unchanged-HEAD ordinary failure, and retains the marker with the exact frozen diagnostic after a produced mismatch without changing FR-090 or FR-221 denial bytes
- Verification-expansion smoke on a fixture repo: assertion sensor + invariant gate → fast docs commit with independent guard recomputation → standard promotion → advisory mutation merge → mechanical/semantic drift report → ungated run logging and reporting
- Opt-in live smoke (real Codex CLI): one `codex exec` launch per distinct configured model (`gpt-5.6-sol`) verifying model acceptance and the effort config key at each configured effort
- Phase-3 smoke: registered worktree → merge start → Gate 1/advisory mutation/Gate 2 → unconditional review-final → control approval when applicable → bounded locked rebase/final mechanical checks → exact ordinary non-force push → durable pushed truth → non-force cleanup
- Phase-4 close smoke: `bd dolt push` → `python3 "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/forge/cli.py" push` → `git status`, with short-circuit failure; activated raw commit/push denial while all three sanctioned CLI producer paths remain legal; legal no-provenance default-branch push; and invalid-mode repair
- Every required serial suite record is judged from that suite's own output tail, never a background wrapper's exit code; after the last defect fix, applicable end-to-end checks follow FR-121 and full unittest discovery passes twice consecutively before phase-5 admission can be claimed

## 12. Success Criteria

- **SC-001**: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tests` exits 0 from the plugin root on macOS and Linux with bare Python ≥ 3.10 (no packages installed).
- **SC-002**: Structural `validate` accepts parseable JSON objects with unknown or legacy kinds; malformed JSON lines fail with the structural diagnostic, and no result gates a chain or run operation.
- **SC-004**: On a freshly initialized scaffold repo before region filling, `grep -rln "forge-init:" forge-project.md` is non-empty and the Gate 1 command exits 1; after `/forge:init` completes, the grep is empty and Gate 1 exits 0 on the clean tree.
- **SC-005**: When `HEAD:.forge-manifest` contains anchored `^plugin_ref: ` or a worktree manifest exists that does not parse as upstream schema, absence of `.forge/tmp/authorized/<authorization-id>` makes the guard deny `git commit` — including `git -C <path> commit` and `cd x && git commit`; a fresh exact v2 marker whose filename/ID/object-format/tree match the invoking index allows, while a legacy marker never does, an upstream-only manifest remains halt-only, and staged deletion of a committed plugin manifest stays armed. With `AGENT_HALT` present it denies both `git commit` and `git push` in any repo.
- **SC-006**: `codex execpolicy check --rules .codex/rules/forge.rules` reports decision `forbidden` for `git push --force`, `git push origin HEAD`, `git reset --hard`, `git clean -fd`, and `rm -rf /`.
- **SC-007**: `run-evals.sh` on an empty task dir exits 2; on the three seeded fixtures with matching baselines exits 0; flipping one baseline exits 1.
- **SC-008**: `grep -ri opencode` over the plugin tree returns matches only under `UPSTREAM`, `docs/design/`, `docs/specs/`, in `scripts/forge/migrate-upstream.py`, and in tests that exercise that migration reader; it returns no match in any other shipped file. The exception permits migration-time reading required by FR-181, not legacy runtime support.
- **SC-009**: The E2E smoke run (fixture repo, `fake_codex.py`) completes twice consecutively with candidate-bound gates and independent review passing, per FR-121, then records a free-text run outcome without a close judgment.
- **SC-010**: A working-tree edit that replaces a committed invariant or tier policy cannot change any hook/gate command or authorize a fast commit; invariant timeouts kill the full process group and cap combined output at 65,536 bytes.
- **SC-011**: A docs-only fast commit completes without Step 4 review only when the classifier and guard independently derive fast from the exact immutable tree/path evidence and the same committed `risk-tiers`/`trigger-paths`/`file-categories` source; Python de-indent, YAML nesting, dependency-block matches, and unknown-stack manifests derive at least standard, and policy/eligibility drift produces the exact FR-154 denial.
- **SC-012**: `drift-check.sh` emits the canonical FR-166 Drift summary schema v1 for exit 0, exit 1, and both exit-2 cases, preserving all thirteen telemetry counters and event-prune outcomes while excluding journal, archive, and learning inputs; semantic reports remain JSON-sourced, append-only, and unaffected by intervening Stop CSV appends, and only CRITICAL findings create the operator-cleared workflow launch block.
- **SC-013**: `/forge:report` describes the original intent, decisions, delivered changes, chain gate evidence, and remaining risks without requiring a run judgment, validated journal references, or committed run archive.
- **SC-014**: Init succeeds with dcg absent, invokes the exact allow command once when needed, skips it on re-init, fills all sixteen regions including the fixed reviewer-facing evaluation trigger table and project-specific guard denylist, creates the retained drift, migration, eval-task, chain, and content-addressed authorization layouts, and remains dependency-free on macOS and Linux.
- **SC-015**: A touched unwaived Python assertion-free test blocks, a non-Python heuristic finding and an absent seeded heuristic remain advisory, and a valid `# forge-assertion-waiver: <reason>` suppresses only its containing file while remaining visible in review evidence; the resulting blocking/advisory/waived dispositions and per-reviewer-role finding occurrences map into all thirteen counters and surface in the committed drift report.
- **SC-016**: Upstream migration classifies before mutation, salvages region bodies and imported baselines byte-identically without reminting, blocks activation on reviewer shadow, and commits a complete live-disk migration report before `init_completed: true`.
- **SC-017**: Concurrent commit chains and merges retain marker isolation, worktree ownership, and lock discipline; journals use complete serialized append-only lines without scope admission or session ownership. The real harness loses no Stop-telemetry row or decision-event line, and concurrent decision events retain checked `O_APPEND` writes, registered-writer pruning, and unchanged permission outcomes.
- **SC-019**: With phase 1 live, a commit chain driven only by CLI verbs completes end to end with every evidence record CLI-captured and candidate-bound; the hook dual-accepts chain- and marker-authorized commits with FR-090's denial literals byte-identical; a control-class chain cannot produce a commit without an operator-recorded approval naming the exact candidate; the FR-223 evals pass before any phase-1 surface ships; and disabling any single internal finalize check in memory makes its focused test fail.
- **SC-021**: A clean registered non-main worktree completes the phase-3 CLI path from atomic admission through current Gates 1–4, a bounded locked integration epoch, exact ordinary non-force push after expected-old ancestry proof, durable `pushed` truth, and non-force contained cleanup; every record binds the exact DM-014 generation, no external review/approval occurs under the common lock, and no merge commit, force operation, implicit chain selection, or `merge skip` exists.
- **SC-022**: A remote-only advance retains review/approval only through an immutable generation carry-forward with the exact equality proof and fresh integrated Gates 1–2; any candidate-HEAD rewrite, changed diff/policy, or conflict resolution instead parks outside the lock for fresh Gates 3–4 and renewed control approval naming the integrated full HEAD, while a final mechanical non-PASS leaves the remote untouched.
- **SC-023**: Rebase, push, lock, process, and cleanup crash windows recover from fsynced intent plus authoritative Git/remote observation: exact conflict paths alone may continue; standalone push accepts either the exact already-integrated no-op predicate or a crash-completed rewrite with its exact nonce/action/main-checkout/reflog predicate, while append-only operation results preserve actual failure precedence; attribution ambiguity remains observation-only without changing an actual-failure `rebase-failed` output; unknown push outcome is never blindly retried; current-head containment establishes `pushed`/`closed`, whereas older-only containment records partial `landed_head`, forbids abort/blind retry, and either admits the identity-bound fresh integrated head into history even when its OID deliberately repeats or takes the exact inactive tagged-terminal release without deleting the newer work; cleanup rechecks only current pushed truth; partial non-force cleanup is idempotently retryable without another push; every macOS/Linux entrant contends on the mandatory portable arbiter, standalone push also holds the commit lock, its nonterminal singleton interlocks commit finalize after a crash except for the exact canonical activation repair, invalid mode admits only closed recovery of an existing transaction, and its ordered inner/outer release is recoverable without replay; a proven-dead portable owner/fence is recoverable through the sole provisional reservation; an unprovably live child globally fences every merge/push; and foreign/unclassifiable merge state sets `foreign-git-state` rather than guessing.
- **SC-024**: Before activation, `forge push` is legal and `legacy-v1` preserves phase-1 behavior byte-identically; after committed `forge-verbs-v1`, raw `git commit` and raw `git push` receive the exact denials while `forge commit finalize`, `forge merge finalize`, and `forge push` remain legal, compound raw segments are still denied, malformed mode fails closed but is repairable through the exact approved canonical CLI finalize even from its standalone activation-recovery singleton, any strict-valid nonterminal transaction under invalid mode admits only non-history identity-bound recovery/death proof/owned restoration/observation/release and never new integration or push, every adjacent history-producing tuple remains interlocked, and standalone push never requires chain-produced provenance.
- **SC-025**: Every v1 corpus/eval/manifest byte remains immutable while the exact DM-016 v2 inventory pins the 41-member union, matcher, transition, recovery, push, cleanup, activated-denial, and last-line controls; Revision 7 neither deletes DM-006 nor implements the reserved phase-6 verbs, and a future deletion remains blocked unless all eleven FR-242 predicates independently PASS with binding review and explicit operator approval.

- **SC-030**: The additive v3 corpus pins the complete 53-member `forge-cli/2` union while every v1/v2 row and artefact remains unchanged; reason members retired by Revision 22 remain reserved and are never emitted.
- **SC-035**: Automated worktree cleanup requires fresh branch-tip, attachment, matching-HEAD, cleanliness, and containment proofs, deletes the reverified branch with expected-old-oid `git update-ref -d`, and consults no journal, run-close judgment, or archive dependency.

- **SC-037**: A scripts-only or tests-fixtures-only candidate receives hard review-final at commit and merge without control approval under shipped policy; each built-in floor match is recorded, no project table can narrow the floor, and a consumer's retained control extension still requires approval.
- **SC-038**: A review BLOCK can be fixed by restaging any revised in-repository candidate in the same chain; deterministic delta-trigger carry proofs preserve only unaffected successful conditional evidence, all required fresh gates and review pass before landing, every prior approval is void, and the review cap cannot reset through restage or replacement chains for the same unresolved review.
- **SC-039**: Existing 0.7.1 run directories, journal bytes, activated bindings, and committed archives are never rewritten or migrated; legacy kinds are readable, retired sidecars carry no authority, and the breaking-release documentation names retired verbs and skills.

## 14. Task Decomposition Guidance

1. **Execution logging**: FR-010..FR-013, FR-250..FR-252. Outcome: one append-only journal per run, minimal execution and decision records, tolerant structural reader, and preserved existing bytes.
3. **Governance content** — FR-110..FR-112, FR-120..FR-126, FR-130..FR-132. Outcome: constitution, rules, review-final agent, doctrine woven into workflow/orchestrate skills.
4. **Gate chains + region file** — FR-050..FR-057, FR-060..FR-065, FR-070..FR-073. Outcome: `/forge:commit` and `/forge:worktree-merge` operational against `forge-project.md`.
5. **Installer** — FR-080..FR-085 + system/template + seeds. Outcome: `/forge:init` end-to-end on a scaffold repo, including idempotent dcg detection/allowlisting.
6. **Enforcement + evals** — FR-030..FR-043 launch mechanics in skills, FR-090..FR-095 hooks, FR-100..FR-103 evals. Outcome: guard hook live, execpolicy verified, committed command denylist enforced, evals gating control changes; E2E smoke passes twice.
7. **Invariants + test quality** — FR-139..FR-149 with FR-050/FR-060/FR-073 integration and `system/seeds/validation-snippets/stacks.md`. Outcome: scoped advisory mutation with bounded timeouts/malformed-row evidence, Python-only blocking assertion sensing with narrow waivers, seeded advisory heuristics, and committed-policy invariant checks operate at commit, merge, hook, and drift surfaces.
8. **Risk tiers + decision telemetry** — FR-150..FR-157 with DM-006/FR-054/FR-090 redlines and the required `aggregate-telemetry.sh` events-only CSV output fix. Outcome: deterministic fast/standard/hard routing from shared committed sources, independent fast guard recomputation, safe formatting-only admission, thirteen disposition/reviewer-aware durable-comparison-ready telemetry counters, single-write append atomicity, and bounded lossless emit/prune coordination are verified.
9. **Drift** — FR-160..FR-166 with `/forge:drift`, `drift-staleness.sh`, plugin Stop/SessionStart wiring, and DM-008 report output. Outcome: mechanical + semantic drift loop, schema-v1 summaries, staleness nudge, committed reports, and ignored operator-cleared CRITICAL blocks work end-to-end.
11. **Upstream migration** — FR-180..FR-186 with FR-101 and DM-009. Outcome: schema-directed, byte-preserving migration imports regions/evals, resolves reviewer/Codex/gitignore hazards, and commits a disk-derived migration report before activation.
14. **Forge CLI commit slice** — FR-210..FR-224 with DM-012/DM-013, in phase order: phase 0 lands the FR-223 precondition evals; phase 1 lands `cli.py`, the chain state machine, the output contract, and the hook dual-accept while composing (never rewriting) the existing tested executables; phase 1.1 pins the four shipped grammars without changing behavior; phase 2 switches this repository's own commit chains to the CLI path and watches the fast-tier telemetry. Each implementation phase is its own control-class change. Revision 7 now supplies the later merge/raw-denial boundaries in FR-230..FR-243; their implementation remains in the separate packets below.
15. **Phase-1.1 authority** — DM-003, DM-013, FR-214, FR-216, and §8 only. Outcome: gate IDs, sole changelog mutation grammar, verdict transport including finding-free BLOCK, and canonical fingerprint bytes match shipped HEAD; golden parser tests cover every quirk. This is a spec/control candidate and does not modify runtime behavior.
16. **Merge schema and state authority** — DM-014, FR-230..FR-234, merge portions of §8/§9/scenarios/tests/SC-021..SC-022. Outcome: exact candidate generation, explicit argv, closed enum/transition table, gates, review, approval, and evidence invalidation are independently reviewable before code exists.
17. **Locking and recovery authority** — FR-235..FR-237, recovery portions of §8/§9/scenarios/tests/SC-022..SC-023. Outcome: the substantive FR-062/FR-063 CLI amendment, lock matrix, intent/observation crash law, push truth, cleanup truth, and concurrency contracts are independently reviewable before code exists.
18. **Phase-3 implementation** — DM-014 and FR-230..FR-237 across `cli.py`, worktree-merge routing, state/event persistence, gate/review composition, locks, recovery, chain evidence, v2 reason tests, and real-remote E2E. Outcome: the CLI merge path lands only after all focused checks, STRICT evals, binding review, and explicit operator approval; the old skill contract stays live until cutover.
19. **Phase-4 push, hook, activation, and Beads rendering** — DM-015..DM-017 and FR-238..FR-241 across standalone push, pre-fetch attempt nonces and append-only attempt ledger, singleton recovery and append-only child results, commit-finalize singleton interlock, ordered inner/outer release recovery including condition-bearing fresh attempts and push-derived activation outcomes, invalid-mode nonterminal recovery-only closure, committed-mode parser, quote-aware hook matcher, exact literals, v2 corpora/manifest, init/re-init, installed templates, close-protocol rendering, help/routing inventory, and conformance tests. Outcome: `forge push` ships before opt-in atomic raw denial, legacy behavior remains byte-identical until activation, and every last-line control has a disable-in-memory test.
20. **Future phase-5 deletion** — FR-242 only after a new control revision mechanically proves all eleven predicates. Outcome: a separately reviewed deletion candidate preserves supported-repository upgrade safety; Revision 7 and phase 4 retain every marker/parser/test/v1 byte.
21. **Future phase-6 ordering** — FR-243. Outcome: a later spec defines the complete state, canonical digests, path/concurrency/recovery law, chain evidence, reasons, tests, and authority before implementing the two reserved verbs; the claim remains best-effort ordering, never confidentiality.

28. **Record-keeping simplification implementation**: FR-250..FR-256 and all amended surviving contracts. Outcome: remove close judgment, scope registry, binding/outbox/ingest, route freeze, archive production, and learning surfaces; retain exact chain evidence, independent review, approval and lock controls; ship the built-in final-review floor and deterministic same-chain fixes; verify 0.7.1 compatibility without rewriting existing records or history.
